| `rv_w`        | `waaddu_ww`            | `wm, ws2, ws1, wd`   | `RV-W 2D adaptation in row-major order (p=i*COLS+j; COLS is the column extent of the accessed view). :RVW - Specific Extension for Matrix Operations

| **Extension**      | **Name**                  | **Variable-Fields**             | **Description**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
